    45  function normalizeBoard(value) {
    46    const parsed = parseJson(value, []);
    47    const board = Array.from({ length: BOARD_SIZE }, (_, index) => {
    48      const cell = parsed[index];
    49      return cell === '' || cell === undefined ? null : cell;
    50    });
    51    return board;
    52  }
    53  
    54  function parseJson(value, fallback) {
    55    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '' || value ===
'none') {
    56      return fallback;
    57    }
    58    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    59      return value;
    60    }
    61    try {
    62      return JSON.parse(value);
    63    } catch {
    64      return fallback;
    65    }
    66  }
    67  
    68  function stringifyJson(value) {
    69    return JSON.stringify(value);
    70  }
    71  
    72  function toStoredBoard(board) {
    73    return stringifyJson(board.map((cell) => (cell === null ? null : 
cell)));
    74  }
    75  
    76  function toStoredMoves(moves) {
    77    return stringifyJson(moves.map((move) => ({
    78      color: move.color,
    79      column: move.column,
    80      row: move.row,
    81      index: move.index,
    82    })));
    83  }
    84  
    85  function toStoredCells(cells) {
    86    return stringifyJson(cells);
    87  }
    88  
    89  function oppositeColor(color) {
    90    return color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
    91  }
    92  
    93  function boardIndex(row, column) {
    94    return (row - 1) * BOARD_WIDTH + (column - 1);
    95  }
    96  
    97  function columnHasSpace(board, column) {
    98    const zeroBased = column - 1;
    99    for (let row = BOARD_HEIGHT; row >= 1; row -= 1) {
   100      if (board[boardIndex(row, column)] === null) {
   101        return true;
   102      }
   103    }
   104    return false;
   105  }
   106  
   107  function findLandingIndex(board, column) {
   108    for (let row = BOARD_HEIGHT; row >= 1; row -= 1) {
   109      const index = boardIndex(row, column);
   110      if (board[index] === null) {
   111        return { row, index };
   112      }
   113    }
   114    return null;
   115  }
   116  
   117  function collectLine(board, startIndex, color, deltaRow, deltaColumn) {
   118    const startRow = Math.floor(startIndex / BOARD_WIDTH) + 1;
   119    const startColumn = (startIndex % BOARD_WIDTH) + 1;
   120    const cells = [{ row: startRow, column: startColumn, index: startIndex
}];
   121  
   122    let row = startRow - deltaRow;
   123    let column = startColumn - deltaColumn;
   124    while (row >= 1 && row <= BOARD_HEIGHT && column >= 1 && column <= 
BOARD_WIDTH) {
   125      const index = boardIndex(row, column);
   126      if (board[index] !== color) {
   127        break;
   128      }
   129      cells.unshift({ row, column, index });
   130      row -= deltaRow;
   131      column -= deltaColumn;
   132    }
   133  
   134    row = startRow + deltaRow;
   135    column = startColumn + deltaColumn;
   136    while (row >= 1 && row <= BOARD_HEIGHT && column >= 1 && column <= 
BOARD_WIDTH) {
   137      const index = boardIndex(row, column);
   138      if (board[index] !== color) {
   139        break;
   140      }
   141      cells.push({ row, column, index });
   142      row += deltaRow;
   143      column += deltaColumn;
   144    }
   145  
   146    return cells;
   147  }
   148  
   149  function winningCellsFor(board, startIndex, color) {
   150    const directions = [
   151      [0, 1],
   152      [1, 0],
   153      [1, 1],
   154      [1, -1],
   155    ];
   156  
   157    for (const [deltaRow, deltaColumn] of directions) {
   158      const line = collectLine(board, startIndex, color, deltaRow, 
deltaColumn);
   159      if (line.length >= 4) {
   160        const pivot = line.findIndex((cell) => cell.index === startIndex);
   161        let start = Math.max(0, pivot - 3);
   162        if (start + 4 > line.length) {
   163          start = line.length - 4;
   164        }
   165        return line.slice(start, start + 4).map((cell) => cell.index);
   166      }
   167    }
   168  
   169    return [];
   170  }
   171  
   172  function boardFull(board) {
   173    return board.every((cell) => cell !== null);
   174  }
   175  
   176  function parseHistoryList(value) {
   177    return parseJson(value, []).map((entry) => ({
   178      color: entry.color,
   179      column: entry.column,
   180      row: entry.row,
   181      index: entry.index,
   182    }));
   183  }
   184  
   185  function parseArchiveSnapshot(value) {
   186    const snapshot = parseJson(value, null);
   187    if (!snapshot) {
   188      return null;
   189    }
   190    return {
   191      matchId: snapshot.matchId,
   192      roundId: snapshot.roundId,
   193      result: snapshot.result,
   194      finalBoard: snapshot.finalBoard,
   195      moves: parseHistoryList(snapshot.moves),
   196      winningCells: snapshot.winningCells || [],
   197      moveCount: snapshot.moveCount,
   198      completedAt: snapshot.completedAt,
   199    };
   200  }
   201  
   202  function serializeArchiveSnapshot(snapshot) {
   203    return stringifyJson({
   204      matchId: snapshot.matchId,
   205      roundId: snapshot.roundId,
   206      result: snapshot.result,
   207      finalBoard: snapshot.finalBoard,
   208      moves: snapshot.moves,
   209      winningCells: snapshot.winningCells,
   210      moveCount: snapshot.moveCount,
   211      completedAt: snapshot.completedAt,
   212    });
   213  }
   214  
   215  function parseGameRow(row) {
   216    return {
   217      accountId: row.account_id,
   218      roundId: row.round_id,
   219      board: normalizeBoard(row.board),
   220      currentPlayer: row.current_player,
   221      status: row.status,
   222      result: row.result,
   223      winningCells: parseJson(row.winning_cells, []),
   224      redWins: row.red_wins,
   225      yellowWins: row.yellow_wins,
   226      draws: row.draws,
   227      revision: row.revision,
   228      appliedHistory: parseHistoryList(row.applied_history),
   229      redoHistory: parseHistoryList(row.redo_history),
   230      terminalSnapshot: parseArchiveSnapshot(row.terminal_snapshot),
   231    };
   232  }
   233  
   234  function buildArchiveSummary(db, accountId) {
   235    const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM 
completed_matches WHERE account_id = ?').get(accountId).count;
   236    const latest = db.prepare(`
   237      SELECT match_id, round_id, result, move_count, completed_at
   238      FROM completed_matches
   239      WHERE account_id = ?
   240      ORDER BY datetime(completed_at) DESC, id DESC
   241      LIMIT 10
   242    `).all(accountId).map((row) => ({
   243      matchId: row.match_id,
   244      roundId: row.round_id,
   245      result: row.result,
   246      moveCount: row.move_count,
   247      completedAt: row.completed_at,
   248    }));
   249    return { count, latest };
   250  }
   251  
   252  function buildStateBundle(db, accountId, feedback = '') {
   253    const account = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name FROM accounts WHERE
id = ?').get(accountId);
   254    const gameRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_state WHERE account_id 
= ?').get(accountId);
   255    if (!account || !gameRow) {
   256      return null;
   257    }
   258    const game = parseGameRow(gameRow);
   259    const archive = buildArchiveSummary(db, accountId);
   260    return {
   261      account: {
   262        id: account.id,
   263        email: account.email,
   264        name: account.name,
   265      },
   266      feedback,
   267      revision: game.revision,
   268      roundId: game.roundId,
   269      status: game.status,
   270      currentPlayer: game.currentPlayer,
   271      currentTurnLabel: game.status === 'active' ? 
`${game.currentPlayer}'s turn` : game.result,
   272      result: game.result,
   273      board: game.board,
   274      winningCells: game.winningCells,
   275      totals: {
   276        redWins: game.redWins,
   277        yellowWins: game.yellowWins,
   278        draws: game.draws,
   279      },
   280      appliedHistory: game.appliedHistory,
   281      redoHistory: game.redoHistory,
   282      archive,
   283    };
   284  }
   285  
   286  function loadArchiveMatch(db, accountId, matchId) {
   287    const row = db.prepare(`
   288      SELECT *
   289      FROM completed_matches
   290      WHERE account_id = ? AND match_id = ?
   291    `).get(accountId, matchId);
   292    if (!row) {
   293      return null;
   294    }
   295    return {
   296      matchId: row.match_id,
   297      roundId: row.round_id,
   298      result: row.result,
   299      finalBoard: normalizeBoard(row.final_board),
   300      moves: parseHistoryList(row.moves),
   301      winningCells: parseJson(row.winning_cells, []),
   302      moveCount: row.move_count,
   303      completedAt: row.completed_at,
   304    };
   305  }

   307  function ensureTables(db) {
   308    db.exec(`
   309      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS accounts (
   310        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   311        email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   312        name TEXT NOT NULL,
   313        password_salt TEXT NOT NULL,
   314        password_hash TEXT NOT NULL
   315      );
   316  
   317      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_tokens (
   318        token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   319        account_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
   320        issued_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   321        last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   322        FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
   323      );
   324  
   325      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS game_state (
   326        account_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
   327        round_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   328        board TEXT NOT NULL,
   329        current_player TEXT NOT NULL,
   330        status TEXT NOT NULL,
   331        result TEXT NOT NULL,
   332        winning_cells TEXT NOT NULL,
   333        red_wins INTEGER NOT NULL,
   334        yellow_wins INTEGER NOT NULL,
   335        draws INTEGER NOT NULL,
   336        revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
   337        applied_history TEXT NOT NULL,
   338        redo_history TEXT NOT NULL,
   339        terminal_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
   340        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   341        FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
   342      );
   343  
   344      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS completed_matches (
   345        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   346        account_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
   347        match_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   348        round_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   349        result TEXT NOT NULL,
   350        final_board TEXT NOT NULL,
   351        winning_cells TEXT NOT NULL,
   352        moves TEXT NOT NULL,
   353        move_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
   354        completed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   355        FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
   356      );
   357  
   358      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operations (
   359        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   360        account_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
   361        operation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   362        mutation_type TEXT NOT NULL,
   363        request_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
   364        response_status INTEGER NOT NULL,
   365        response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
   366        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   367        UNIQUE (account_id, operation_id),
   368        FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
   369      );
   370    `);
   371  }

   403        const info = insertAccount.run(row.Email, row.Name, salt, hash);
   404        accountIds.set(row.Email, info.lastInsertRowid);

   414        const yellowWins = row['Yellow wins'];
   415        const draws = row['Draws'];
   416        const appliedHistory = parseHistoryList(row['Applied history']);
   417        const redoHistory = parseHistoryList(row['Redo history']);
   418        insertState.run(
   419          accountId,
   420          row['Round id'],

   462  function openDatabase() {
   463    const dbExists = fs.existsSync(DB_PATH);
   464    const db = new Database(DB_PATH);
   465    db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
   466    db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
   467    db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
   468    ensureTables(db);
   469    const accountCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM 
accounts').get().count;
   470    if (!dbExists || accountCount === 0) {
   471      db.exec('DELETE FROM operations; DELETE FROM active_tokens; DELETE 
FROM completed_matches; DELETE FROM game_state; DELETE FROM accounts;');
   472      seedDatabase(db);
   473    }
   474    return db;
   475  }

   479  function authenticateRequest(req, res, next) {
   480    const header = req.headers.authorization || '';
   481    const [scheme, token] = header.split(' ');
   482    if (scheme !== 'Bearer' || !token) {
   483      return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' });
   484    }
   485    const tokenRow = db.prepare(`
   486      SELECT active_tokens.account_id, accounts.email, accounts.name
   487      FROM active_tokens
   488      JOIN accounts ON accounts.id = active_tokens.account_id
   489      WHERE active_tokens.token = ?
   490    `).get(token);
   491    if (!tokenRow) {
   492      return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' });
   493    }
   494    db.prepare('UPDATE active_tokens SET last_seen_at = ? WHERE token = 
?').run(nowIso(), token);
   495    req.auth = {
   496      token,
   497      accountId: tokenRow.account_id,
   498      account: {
   499        id: tokenRow.account_id,
   500        email: tokenRow.email,
   501        name: tokenRow.name,
   502      },
   503    };
   504    return next();
   505  }

   507  function storeOperation(accountId, operationId, mutationType, 
requestRevision, responseStatus, responseBody) {
   508    db.prepare(`
   509      INSERT INTO operations (
   510        account_id, operation_id, mutation_type, request_revision, 
response_status, response_body, created_at
   511      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   512    `).run(accountId, operationId, mutationType, requestRevision, 
responseStatus, JSON.stringify(responseBody), nowIso());
   513  }
   514  
   515  function getStoredOperation(accountId, operationId) {
   516    const row = db.prepare(`
   517      SELECT response_status, response_body
   518      FROM operations
   519      WHERE account_id = ? AND operation_id = ?
   520    `).get(accountId, operationId);
   521    if (!row) {
   522      return null;
   523    }
   524    return {
   525      status: row.response_status,
   526      body: JSON.parse(row.response_body),
   527    };
   528  }
   529  
   530  function mutationTransaction(accountId, body, mutationType, handler) {
   531    const operationId = typeof body.operationId === 'string' ? 
body.operationId.trim() : '';
   532    const expectedRevision = Number(body.expectedRevision);
   533    if (!operationId) {
   534      return { status: 400, body: { ok: false, message: 'Missing operation
id' } };
   535    }
   536  
   537    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
   538      const stored = getStoredOperation(accountId, operationId);
   539      if (stored) {
   540        return stored;
   541      }
   542  
   543      const currentRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_state WHERE 
account_id = ?').get(accountId);
   544      if (!currentRow) {
   545        const response = { ok: false, message: 'Account state not found' 
};
   546        storeOperation(accountId, operationId, mutationType, 
Number.isFinite(expectedRevision) ? expectedRevision : -1, 500, response);
   547        return { status: 500, body: response };
   548      }
   549  
   550      const current = parseGameRow(currentRow);
   551      if (Number.isNaN(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision !== 
current.revision) {
   552        const response = {
   553          ok: false,
   554          message: 'Game updated in another tab',
   555          state: buildStateBundle(db, accountId, 'Game updated in another 
tab'),
   556        };
   557        storeOperation(accountId, operationId, mutationType, 
Number.isNaN(expectedRevision) ? -1 : expectedRevision, 409, response);
   558        return { status: 409, body: response };
   559      }
   560  
   561      const response = handler({ currentRow, current, accountId, 
operationId, mutationType });
   562      storeOperation(accountId, operationId, mutationType, 
expectedRevision, response.status, response.body);
   563      return response;
   564    });
   565  
   566    return tx();
   567  }
   568  
   569  function updateGameState(accountId, fields) {
   570    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_state WHERE account_id
= ?').get(accountId);
   571    if (!existing) {
   572      throw new Error('Game state missing');
   573    }
   574    const next = {
   575      round_id: fields.roundId ?? existing.round_id,
   576      board: fields.board ?? existing.board,
   577      current_player: fields.currentPlayer ?? existing.current_player,
   578      status: fields.status ?? existing.status,
   579      result: fields.result ?? existing.result,
   580      winning_cells: fields.winningCells ?? existing.winning_cells,
   581      red_wins: fields.redWins ?? existing.red_wins,
   582      yellow_wins: fields.yellowWins ?? existing.yellow_wins,
   583      draws: fields.draws ?? existing.draws,
   584      revision: fields.revision ?? existing.revision,
   585      applied_history: fields.appliedHistory ?? existing.applied_history,
   586      redo_history: fields.redoHistory ?? existing.redo_history,
   587      terminal_snapshot: fields.terminalSnapshot ?? 
existing.terminal_snapshot,
   588    };
   589    db.prepare(`
   590      UPDATE game_state
   591      SET round_id = ?, board = ?, current_player = ?, status = ?, result 
= ?, winning_cells = ?,
   592          red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, revision = ?, 
applied_history = ?, redo_history = ?,
   593          terminal_snapshot = ?, updated_at = ?
   594      WHERE account_id = ?
   595    `).run(
   596      next.round_id,
   597      next.board,
   598      next.current_player,
   599      next.status,
   600      next.result,

   602      next.red_wins,
   603      next.yellow_wins,
   604      next.draws,

   606      next.applied_history,
   607      next.redo_history,
   608      next.terminal_snapshot,
   609      nowIso(),
   610      accountId,
   611    );

   640  function sendState(res, accountId, feedback = '') {
   641    const bundle = buildStateBundle(db, accountId, feedback);
   642    if (!bundle) {
   643      return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: 'not_found' });
   644    }
   645    return res.json({ ok: true, state: bundle });
   646  }

   657    const email = typeof req.body?.email === 'string' ? 
req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
   658    const password = typeof req.body?.password === 'string' ? 
req.body.password : '';
   659    const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE lower(email) 
= ?').get(email);
   660    if (!account || !verifyPassword(password, account.password_salt, 
account.password_hash)) {

   666      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
   667    `).run(token, account.id, nowIso(), nowIso());
   668    const state = buildStateBundle(db, account.id, '');
   669    return res.json({ ok: true, token, state });

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/app.js"
  file_text: 
    const BOARD_WIDTH = 7;
    const BOARD_HEIGHT = 6;
    const BOARD_SIZE = BOARD_WIDTH * BOARD_HEIGHT;
    
    const appState = {
      token: localStorage.getItem('droplineToken') || '',
      state: null,
      pending: false,
      pendingColumn: null,
      replay: {
        match: null,
        step: 0,
        opener: null,
      },
    };
    
    const dom = {
      headerChip: document.getElementById('headerChip'),
      signInView: document.getElementById('signInView'),
      gameView: document.getElementById('gameView'),
      signInForm: document.getElementById('signInForm'),
      emailInput: document.getElementById('emailInput'),
      passwordInput: document.getElementById('passwordInput'),
      signInFeedback: document.getElementById('signInFeedback'),
      accountName: document.getElementById('accountName'),
      accountEmail: document.getElementById('accountEmail'),
      turnLabel: document.getElementById('turnLabel'),
      revisionLabel: document.getElementById('revisionLabel'),
      roundLabel: document.getElementById('roundLabel'),
      stateFeedback: document.getElementById('stateFeedback'),
      redWinsLabel: document.getElementById('redWinsLabel'),
      yellowWinsLabel: document.getElementById('yellowWinsLabel'),
      drawsLabel: document.getElementById('drawsLabel'),
      newGameButton: document.getElementById('newGameButton'),
      undoButton: document.getElementById('undoButton'),
      redoButton: document.getElementById('redoButton'),
      signOutButton: document.getElementById('signOutButton'),
      columnControls: document.getElementById('columnControls'),
      boardGrid: document.getElementById('boardGrid'),
      historyList: document.getElementById('historyList'),
      archiveSummary: document.getElementById('archiveSummary'),
      archiveList: document.getElementById('archiveList'),
      replayModal: document.getElementById('replayModal'),
      replayTitle: document.getElementById('replayTitle'),
      replayMeta: document.getElementById('replayMeta'),
      replayBoard: document.getElementById('replayBoard'),
      replayRange: document.getElementById('replayRange'),
      replayStepLabel: document.getElementById('replayStepLabel'),
      replayPreviousButton: document.getElementById('replayPreviousButton'),
      replayNextButton: document.getElementById('replayNextButton'),
      closeReplayButton: document.getElementById('closeReplayButton'),
    };
    
    function boardIndex(row, column) {
      return (row - 1) * BOARD_WIDTH + (column - 1);
    }
    
    function cellState(board, index) {
      const value = board[index];
      return value === null || value === '' ? 'empty' : value;
    }
    
    function formatDate(value) {
      const date = new Date(value);
      if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return value;
      }
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
        dateStyle: 'medium',
        timeStyle: 'short',
      }).format(date);
    }
    
    function escapeText(value) {
      return String(value)
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    
    function focusColumnButton(column) {
      const button = 
dom.columnControls.querySelector(`[data-column="${column}"]`);
      if (button) {
        button.focus();
      }
    }
    
    function setAuthMessage(message = '') {
      dom.signInFeedback.textContent = message;
    }
    
    function setStateMessage(message = '') {
      if (appState.state) {
        appState.state.feedback = message;
      }
      dom.stateFeedback.textContent = message;
    }
    
    function setPending(nextPending, column = null) {
      appState.pending = nextPending;
      appState.pendingColumn = column;
      syncButtonStates();
    }
    
    function syncButtonStates() {
      const hasState = Boolean(appState.state);
      const hasUndo = hasState && appState.state.appliedHistory.length > 0;
      const hasRedo = hasState && appState.state.redoHistory.length > 0;
      const terminal = hasState && appState.state.status === 'terminal';
    
      dom.newGameButton.disabled = !hasState || appState.pending;
      dom.undoButton.disabled = !hasUndo || appState.pending;
      dom.redoButton.disabled = !hasRedo || appState.pending;
      dom.signOutButton.disabled = !hasState || appState.pending;
    
      for (const button of 
dom.columnControls.querySelectorAll('button[data-column]')) {
        button.disabled = !hasState || appState.pending;
        button.setAttribute('aria-disabled', terminal ? 'true' : 'false');
      }
    
      dom.replayPreviousButton.disabled = !appState.replay.match || 
appState.replay.step <= 0;
      dom.replayNextButton.disabled = !appState.replay.match || 
appState.replay.step >= (appState.replay.match ? 
appState.replay.match.moves.length : 0);
      dom.replayRange.disabled = !appState.replay.match;
    }
    
    function renderColumnControls() {
      dom.columnControls.replaceChildren();
      for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_WIDTH; column += 1) {
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.className = 'column-button';
        button.dataset.column = String(column);
        button.innerHTML = `<span>Drop in</span><span>column ${column}</span>`;
        button.setAttribute('aria-label', `Drop in column ${column}`);
        button.addEventListener('click', () => {
          dropPiece(column, button);
        });
        button.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
          if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (column > 1) {
              focusColumnButton(column - 1);
            }
            return;
          }
          if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (column < BOARD_WIDTH) {
              focusColumnButton(column + 1);
            }
            return;
          }
          if (event.key === 'Home') {
            event.preventDefault();
            focusColumnButton(1);
            return;
          }
          if (event.key === 'End') {
            event.preventDefault();
            focusColumnButton(BOARD_WIDTH);
            return;
          }
          if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
            event.preventDefault();
            dropPiece(column, button);
          }
        });
        dom.columnControls.appendChild(button);
      }
    }
    
    function renderBoardCells(container, board, winningCells = [], labelPrefix =
'') {
      container.replaceChildren();
      for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_HEIGHT; row += 1) {
        for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_WIDTH; column += 1) {
          const index = boardIndex(row, column);
          const value = cellState(board, index);
          const isWinning = winningCells.includes(index);
          const cell = document.createElement('div');
          cell.className = 'cell';
          cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
          if (isWinning) {
            cell.classList.add('winning');
          }
          const prefix = labelPrefix ? `${labelPrefix} ` : '';
          cell.setAttribute(
            'aria-label',
            `${prefix}row ${row}, column ${column}, ${value}${isWinning ? ', 
winning' : ''}`,
          );
          const piece = document.createElement('span');
          piece.className = 'piece';
          if (value === 'Red') {
            piece.classList.add('red');
          } else if (value === 'Yellow') {
            piece.classList.add('yellow');
          }
          cell.appendChild(piece);
          container.appendChild(cell);
        }
      }
    }
    
    function renderHistory(history) {
      dom.historyList.replaceChildren();
      if (!history.length) {
        const item = document.createElement('li');
        item.textContent = 'No moves yet.';
        dom.historyList.appendChild(item);
        return;
      }
      history.forEach((move, index) => {
        const item = document.createElement('li');
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'history-row';
        const meta = document.createElement('div');
        meta.className = 'history-meta';
        const title = document.createElement('div');
        title.className = 'history-title';
        title.textContent = `Move ${index + 1}: ${move.color}, column 
${move.column}, row ${move.row}`;
        const detail = document.createElement('div');
        detail.textContent = `Landing cell index ${move.index + 1}`;
        meta.append(title, detail);
        row.appendChild(meta);
        item.appendChild(row);
        dom.historyList.appendChild(item);
      });
    }
    
    function renderArchive(archive) {
      dom.archiveSummary.textContent = `${archive.count} archived 
match${archive.count === 1 ? '' : 'es'}. Latest ten are shown first.`;
      dom.archiveList.replaceChildren();
      if (!archive.latest.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement('div');
        empty.className = 'archive-item';
        empty.textContent = 'No completed matches yet.';
        dom.archiveList.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }
    
      archive.latest.forEach((match) => {
        const item = document.createElement('div');
        item.className = 'archive-item';
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'archive-row';
    
        const meta = document.createElement('div');
        meta.className = 'archive-meta';
        const title = document.createElement('div');
        title.className = 'archive-title';
        title.textContent = `${match.matchId} · ${match.result} · 
${match.moveCount} move${match.moveCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
        const detail = document.createElement('div');
        detail.textContent = formatDate(match.completedAt);
        meta.append(title, detail);
    
        const actions = document.createElement('div');
        actions.className = 'archive-actions';
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.className = 'archive-button';
        button.textContent = 'Open replay';
        button.addEventListener('click', () => {
          openReplay(match.matchId, button);
        });
        actions.appendChild(button);
    
        row.append(meta, actions);
        item.appendChild(row);
        dom.archiveList.appendChild(item);
      });
    }
    
    function renderGameState(state) {
      dom.signInView.hidden = true;
      dom.gameView.hidden = false;
      dom.accountName.textContent = state.account.name;
      dom.accountEmail.textContent = state.account.email;
      dom.headerChip.textContent = `Revision ${state.revision} · Round 
${state.roundId}`;
      dom.turnLabel.textContent = state.currentTurnLabel;
      dom.revisionLabel.textContent = String(state.revision);
      dom.roundLabel.textContent = state.roundId;
      dom.redWinsLabel.textContent = String(state.totals.redWins);
      dom.yellowWinsLabel.textContent = String(state.totals.yellowWins);
      dom.drawsLabel.textContent = String(state.totals.draws);
      setStateMessage(state.feedback || '');
      dom.newGameButton.disabled = appState.pending;
      dom.undoButton.disabled = appState.pending || state.appliedHistory.length 
=== 0;
      dom.redoButton.disabled = appState.pending || state.redoHistory.length ===
0;
      dom.signOutButton.disabled = appState.pending;
      renderBoardCells(dom.boardGrid, state.board, state.winningCells || [], 
'');
      renderHistory(state.appliedHistory);
      renderArchive(state.archive);
      syncButtonStates();
    }
    
    function showSignIn(message = '') {
      dom.signInView.hidden = false;
      dom.gameView.hidden = true;
      dom.headerChip.textContent = 'Sign in to continue';
      setAuthMessage(message);
      dom.passwordInput.value = '';
      dom.signInForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = false;
    }
    
    async function apiRequest(path, { method = 'GET', body = null, auth = true }
= {}) {
      const headers = {
        Accept: 'application/json',
      };
      if (body !== null) {
        headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
      }
      if (auth && appState.token) {
        headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${appState.token}`;
      }
      const response = await fetch(path, {
        method,
        headers,
        body: body === null ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
      });
      let data = {};
      try {
        data = await response.json();
      } catch {
        data = {};
      }
      if (response.status === 401 && auth) {
        handleUnauthorized();
      }
      return { response, data };
    }
    
    function handleUnauthorized(message = 'Your session ended. Please sign in 
again.') {
      localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
      appState.token = '';
      appState.state = null;
      appState.pending = false;
      appState.pendingColumn = null;
      appState.replay.match = null;
      appState.replay.step = 0;
      appState.replay.opener = null;
      dom.replayModal.hidden = true;
      renderColumnControls();
      showSignIn(message);
    }
    
    function applyState(state) {
      appState.state = state;
      if (appState.token) {
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', appState.token);
      }
      renderGameState(state);
    }
    
    async function loadState() {
      if (!appState.token) {
        showSignIn();
        renderColumnControls();
        syncButtonStates();
        return;
      }
      setAuthMessage('Restoring your saved game...');
      const { response, data } = await apiRequest('/api/state');
      if (!response.ok) {
        if (response.status !== 401) {
          setAuthMessage(data.message || 'Unable to restore the game.');
        }
        return;
      }
      applyState(data.state);
    }
    
    async function signIn(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      const email = dom.emailInput.value.trim();
      const password = dom.passwordInput.value;
      setAuthMessage('Signing in...');
      dom.signInForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = true;
      const { response, data } = await apiRequest('/api/sign-in', {
        method: 'POST',
        auth: false,
        body: { email, password },
      });
      dom.signInForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = false;
      if (!response.ok) {
        showSignIn(data.message || 'Sign-in failed.');
        return;
      }
      appState.token = data.token;
      localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', data.token);
      appState.replay.match = null;
      appState.replay.step = 0;
      applyState(data.state);
      dom.passwordInput.value = '';
    }
    
    async function signOut() {
      if (!appState.token || appState.pending) {
        return;
      }
      setPending(true);
      const { response } = await apiRequest('/api/sign-out', { method: 'POST' 
});
      setPending(false);
      if (response.status === 401) {
        return;
      }
      handleUnauthorized('You signed out successfully.');
    }
    
    async function submitMutation(path, payload, focusColumn = null) {
      if (!appState.state || appState.pending) {
        return;
      }
      const nextPayload = {
        ...payload,
        expectedRevision: appState.state.revision,
        operationId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      };
      setPending(true, focusColumn);
      const { response, data } = await apiRequest(path, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: nextPayload,
      });
      setPending(false, null);
    
      if (response.status === 401) {
        return;
      }
    
      if (data.state) {
        applyState(data.state);
      }
    
      if (!response.ok) {
        if (data.message) {
          setStateMessage(data.message);
        }
      } else {
        setStateMessage(data.state?.feedback || '');
      }
    
      if (focusColumn !== null) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          focusColumnButton(focusColumn);
        });
      }
    }
    
    async function dropPiece(column, button) {
      await submitMutation('/api/move', { column }, column);
      if (button && appState.state && appState.state.status === 'active') {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => button.focus());
      }
    }
    
    async function newGame() {
      await submitMutation('/api/new-game', {});
    }
    
    async function undoMove() {
      await submitMutation('/api/undo', {});
    }
    
    async function redoMove() {
      await submitMutation('/api/redo', {});
    }
    
    function replayBoardAtStep(match, step) {
      const board = Array.from({ length: BOARD_SIZE }, () => null);
      for (let index = 0; index < step; index += 1) {
        const move = match.moves[index];
        board[move.index] = move.color;
      }
      return board;
    }
    
    function renderReplay(match, step) {
      const safeStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(step, match.moves.length));
      const board = replayBoardAtStep(match, safeStep);
      const winningCells = safeStep === match.moves.length ? (match.winningCells
|| []) : [];
      dom.replayTitle.textContent = match.matchId;
      dom.replayMeta.textContent = `${match.result} · ${match.moveCount} 
move${match.moveCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${formatDate(match.completedAt)}`;
      dom.replayRange.min = '0';
      dom.replayRange.max = String(match.moves.length);
      dom.replayRange.value = String(safeStep);
      dom.replayStepLabel.textContent =
        safeStep === 0 ? 'Empty step' : `Step ${safeStep} of 
${match.moves.length}`;
      renderBoardCells(dom.replayBoard, board, winningCells, 'Replay');
      dom.replayPreviousButton.disabled = safeStep <= 0;
      dom.replayNextButton.disabled = safeStep >= match.moves.length;
    }
    
    function openReplay(matchId, opener) {
      if (!appState.token || appState.pending) {
        return;
      }
      dom.archiveSummary.textContent = dom.archiveSummary.textContent;
      apiRequest(`/api/archive/${encodeURIComponent(matchId)}`)
        .then(({ response, data }) => {
          if (response.status === 401) {
            return;
          }
          if (!response.ok) {
            setStateMessage(data.message || 'Unable to load the replay.');
            return;
          }
          appState.replay.match = data.match;
          appState.replay.step = data.match.moves.length;
          appState.replay.opener = opener || null;
          dom.replayModal.hidden = false;
          renderReplay(data.match, appState.replay.step);
          syncButtonStates();
          window.setTimeout(() => dom.closeReplayButton.focus(), 0);
        })
        .catch(() => {
          setStateMessage('Unable to load the replay.');
        });
    }
    
    function closeReplay() {
      dom.replayModal.hidden = true;
      const opener = appState.replay.opener;
      appState.replay.match = null;
      appState.replay.step = 0;
      appState.replay.opener = null;
      syncButtonStates();
      if (opener && typeof opener.focus === 'function') {
        opener.focus();
      }
    }
    
    function adjustReplayStep(delta) {
      if (!appState.replay.match) {
        return;
      }
      const nextStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(appState.replay.match.moves.length, 
appState.replay.step + delta));
      appState.replay.step = nextStep;
      renderReplay(appState.replay.match, nextStep);
    }
    
    function wireEvents() {
      dom.signInForm.addEventListener('submit', signIn);
      dom.signOutButton.addEventListener('click', signOut);
      dom.newGameButton.addEventListener('click', newGame);
      dom.undoButton.addEventListener('click', undoMove);
      dom.redoButton.addEventListener('click', redoMove);
      dom.replayPreviousButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
adjustReplayStep(-1));
      dom.replayNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => adjustReplayStep(1));
      dom.replayRange.addEventListener('input', () => {
        if (!appState.replay.match) {
          return;
        }
        appState.replay.step = Number(dom.replayRange.value);
        renderReplay(appState.replay.match, appState.replay.step);
      });
      dom.closeReplayButton.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
      dom.replayModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        if (event.target && event.target.dataset && 
event.target.dataset.closeModal === 'true') {
          closeReplay();
        }
      });
      document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (!dom.replayModal.hidden && event.key === 'Escape') {
          event.preventDefault();
          closeReplay();
        }
      });
    }
    
    async function boot() {
      renderColumnControls();
      wireEvents();
      if (appState.token) {
        await loadState();
      } else {
        showSignIn();
        syncButtonStates();
      }
    }
    
    boot();

+306|async function apiRequest(path, { method = 'GET', body = null, auth = true 
} = {}) {
+307|  const headers = {
+308|    Accept: 'application/json',
+309|  };
+310|  if (body !== null) {
+311|    headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
+312|  }
+313|  if (auth && appState.token) {
+314|    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${appState.token}`;
+315|  }
+316|  const response = await fetch(path, {
+317|    method,
+318|    headers,
+319|    body: body === null ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
+320|  });
+321|  let data = {};
+322|  try {
+323|    data = await response.json();
+324|  } catch {
+325|    data = {};
+326|  }
+327|  if (response.status === 401 && auth) {
+328|    handleUnauthorized();
+329|  }
+330|  return { response, data };
+331|}

+410|async function submitMutation(path, payload, focusColumn = null) {
+411|  if (!appState.state || appState.pending) {
+412|    return;
+413|  }
+414|  const nextPayload = {
+415|    ...payload,
+416|    expectedRevision: appState.state.revision,
+417|    operationId: crypto.randomUUID(),

+421|    method: 'POST',
+422|    body: nextPayload,
+423|  });

+425|

+427|    return;
+428|  }
+429|
+430|  if (data.state) {
+431|    applyState(data.state);
+432|  }
+433|

+435|    if (data.message) {
+436|      setStateMessage(data.message);
+437|    }
+438|  } else {
+439|    setStateMessage(data.state?.feedback || '');
+440|  }
+441|
+442|  if (focusColumn !== null) {
+443|    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+444|      focusColumnButton(focusColumn);
+445|    });
+446|  }

+468|function replayBoardAtStep(match, step) {
+469|  const board = Array.from({ length: BOARD_SIZE }, () => null);
+470|  for (let index = 0; index < step; index += 1) {
+471|    const move = match.moves[index];
+472|    board[move.index] = move.color;
+473|  }
+474|  return board;
+475|}
+476|
+477|function renderReplay(match, step) {
+478|  const safeStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(step, match.moves.length));
+479|  const board = replayBoardAtStep(match, safeStep);
+480|  const winningCells = safeStep === match.moves.length ? 
(match.winningCells || []) : [];
+481|  dom.replayTitle.textContent = match.matchId;
+482|  dom.replayMeta.textContent = `${match.result} · ${match.moveCount} 
move${match.moveCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${formatDate(match.completedAt)}`;
+483|  dom.replayRange.min = '0';
+484|  dom.replayRange.max = String(match.moves.length);
+485|  dom.replayRange.value = String(safeStep);
+486|  dom.replayStepLabel.textContent =
+487|    safeStep === 0 ? 'Empty step' : `Step ${safeStep} of 
${match.moves.length}`;
+488|  renderBoardCells(dom.replayBoard, board, winningCells, 'Replay');
+489|  dom.replayPreviousButton.disabled = safeStep <= 0;
+490|  dom.replayNextButton.disabled = safeStep >= match.moves.length;
+491|}
+492|
+493|function openReplay(matchId, opener) {
+494|  if (!appState.token || appState.pending) {
+495|    return;
+496|  }
+497|  dom.archiveSummary.textContent = dom.archiveSummary.textContent;
+498|  apiRequest(`/api/archive/${encodeURIComponent(matchId)}`)
+499|    .then(({ response, data }) => {
+500|      if (response.status === 401) {
+501|        return;
+502|      }
+503|      if (!response.ok) {
+504|        setStateMessage(data.message || 'Unable to load the replay.');
+505|        return;
+506|      }
+507|      appState.replay.match = data.match;
+508|      appState.replay.step = data.match.moves.length;
+509|      appState.replay.opener = opener || null;
+510|      dom.replayModal.hidden = false;
+511|      renderReplay(data.match, appState.replay.step);
+512|      syncButtonStates();
+513|      window.setTimeout(() => dom.closeReplayButton.focus(), 0);
+514|    })
+515|    .catch(() => {
+516|      setStateMessage('Unable to load the replay.');
+517|    });
+518|}
+519|
+520|function closeReplay() {
+521|  dom.replayModal.hidden = true;
+522|  const opener = appState.replay.opener;
+523|  appState.replay.match = null;
+524|  appState.replay.step = 0;
+525|  appState.replay.opener = null;
+526|  syncButtonStates();
+527|  if (opener && typeof opener.focus === 'function') {
+528|    opener.focus();
+529|  }
+530|}
+531|
+532|function adjustReplayStep(delta) {
+533|  if (!appState.replay.match) {
+534|    return;
+535|  }
+536|  const nextStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(appState.replay.match.moves.length,
appState.replay.step + delta));
+537|  appState.replay.step = nextStep;
+538|  renderReplay(appState.replay.match, nextStep);
+539|}
+540|
+541|function wireEvents() {
+542|  dom.signInForm.addEventListener('submit', signIn);
+543|  dom.signOutButton.addEventListener('click', signOut);
+544|  dom.newGameButton.addEventListener('click', newGame);
+545|  dom.undoButton.addEventListener('click', undoMove);
+546|  dom.redoButton.addEventListener('click', redoMove);
+547|  dom.replayPreviousButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
adjustReplayStep(-1));
+548|  dom.replayNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
adjustReplayStep(1));
+549|  dom.replayRange.addEventListener('input', () => {
+550|    if (!appState.replay.match) {
+551|      return;
+552|    }
+553|    appState.replay.step = Number(dom.replayRange.value);
+554|    renderReplay(appState.replay.match, appState.replay.step);
+555|  });
+556|  dom.closeReplayButton.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
+557|  dom.replayModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+558|    if (event.target && event.target.dataset && 
event.target.dataset.closeModal === 'true') {
+559|      closeReplay();
+560|    }
+561|  });
+562|  document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
+563|    if (!dom.replayModal.hidden && event.key === 'Escape') {
+564|      event.preventDefault();
+565|      closeReplay();
+566|    }
+567|  });
+568|}
+569|
+570|async function boot() {
+571|  renderColumnControls();
+572|  wireEvents();
+573|  if (appState.token) {
+574|    await loadState();
+575|  } else {
+576|    showSignIn();
+577|    syncButtonStates();
+578|  }
+579|}
+580|
+581|boot();

$ node - <<'NODE'
const assert = require('assert');
const fetch = global.fetch;
const base = 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
async function j(path, options={}) {
  const res = await fetch(base + path, options);
  let data = {};
  try { data = await res.json(); } catch {}
  return {res, data};
}
(async () => {
  let r = await j('/api/health');
  console.log('health', r.res.status, r.data);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(r.data.ok, true);

  r = await j('/api/sign-in', {method:'POST', 
headers:{'content-type':'application/json'}, body: 
JSON.stringify({email:'jordan@dropline.test', password:'password123'})});
  console.log('signin jordan', r.res.status, r.data.ok, r.data.state.revision, 
r.data.state.archive.count);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  const token1 = r.data.token;
  assert.ok(token1);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.account.email, 'jordan@dropline.test');
  assert.equal(r.data.state.archive.count, 11);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.board.length, 42);

  r = await j('/api/sign-in', {method:'POST', 
headers:{'content-type':'application/json'}, body: 
JSON.stringify({email:'jordan@dropline.test', password:'password123'})});
  const token2 = r.data.token;
  assert.ok(token2 && token2 !== token1);
  console.log('dual token ok');

  const auth1 = {Authorization:`Bearer ${token1}`, 
'content-type':'application/json'};
  const auth2 = {Authorization:`Bearer ${token2}`, 
'content-type':'application/json'};
  r = await j('/api/state', {headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token1}`}});
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  const state = r.data.state;
  console.log('state', state.revision, state.currentTurnLabel, 
state.appliedHistory.length, state.redoHistory.length);
  assert.equal(state.revision, 4);
  assert.equal(state.currentTurnLabel, "Yellow's turn");

  const nonFullColumn = 1;
  const op1 = 'test-op-1';
  r = await j('/api/move', {method:'POST', headers:auth1, body: 
JSON.stringify({column: nonFullColumn, expectedRevision: state.revision, 
operationId: op1})});
  console.log('move1', r.res.status, r.data.ok, r.data.state.revision, 
r.data.state.currentTurnLabel);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.revision, 5);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.currentTurnLabel, "Red's turn");

  const boardAfterMove = r.data.state.board;
  const op1dup = await j('/api/move', {method:'POST', headers:auth1, body: 
JSON.stringify({column: nonFullColumn, expectedRevision: state.revision, 
operationId: op1})});
  console.log('dup move', op1dup.res.status, op1dup.data.state.revision);
  assert.equal(op1dup.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(op1dup.data.state.revision, 5);
  assert.deepStrictEqual(op1dup.data.state.board, boardAfterMove);

  const stale = await j('/api/move', {method:'POST', headers:auth2, body: 
JSON.stringify({column: 2, expectedRevision: state.revision, operationId: 
'stale-op'})});
  console.log('stale', stale.res.status, stale.data.message);
  assert.equal(stale.res.status, 409);
  assert.equal(stale.data.message, 'Game updated in another tab');
  assert.equal(stale.data.state.revision, 5);

  r = await j('/api/undo', {method:'POST', headers:auth1, body: 
JSON.stringify({expectedRevision: 5, operationId: 'undo-op'})});
  console.log('undo', r.res.status, r.data.state.revision, 
r.data.state.currentTurnLabel);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.revision, 6);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.currentTurnLabel, "Yellow's turn");

  r = await j('/api/redo', {method:'POST', headers:auth1, body: 
JSON.stringify({expectedRevision: 6, operationId: 'redo-op'})});
  console.log('redo', r.res.status, r.data.state.revision, 
r.data.state.currentTurnLabel);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.revision, 7);
  assert.equal(r.data.state.currentTurnLabel, "Red's turn");

  r = await j('/api/archive', {headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token1}`}});
  console.log('archive', r.res.status, r.data.archive.count, 
r.data.archive.latest.length);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.ok(r.data.archive.latest.length <= 10);

  r = await j('/api/archive/seed-jordan-11', {headers:{Authorization:`Bearer 
${token1}`}});
  console.log('replay', r.res.status, r.data.match.matchId, 
r.data.match.moves.length, r.data.match.winningCells.length);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);
  assert.equal(r.data.match.matchId, 'seed-jordan-11');
  assert.equal(r.data.match.moves.length, 8);

  r = await j('/api/sign-out', {method:'POST', headers:{Authorization:`Bearer 
${token1}`}});
  console.log('signout', r.res.status, r.data.ok);
  assert.equal(r.res.status, 200);

  const after = await j('/api/state', {headers:{Authorization:`Bearer 
${token2}`}});
  console.log('after signout other token', after.res.status, after.data.error);
  assert.equal(after.res.status, 401);

  console.log('ALL_OK');
})().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);

- Express + SQLite backend in `server.js`

- sign-in / bad-password feedback
